&lt;project_context>
# 项目: symb_invariant_consensus

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-32608
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westd.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/symb_invariant_consensus


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：下载 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B 模型

**背景**：Director 变更了模型选择，从 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B 改为 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B。原因是 Qwen 底座会被审稿人攻击为"仍是 Qwen 家族"，LLaMA 底座同时提供 reasoning 验证 + 非 Qwen 架构。

**服务器**：westd-32608 (root)
**项目路径**：/root/symb_invariant_consensus
**模型存储**：/root/autodl-tmp/models/

### 步骤

1. SSH 到 westd-32608，检查磁盘空间：`df -h /root/autodl-tmp/`

2. 下载模型（优先 ModelScope，速度更快）：
```bash
cd /root/autodl-tmp/models/
# 优先尝试 ModelScope
pip install modelscope -q 2>/dev/null
python3 -c "
from modelscope import snapshot_download
snapshot_download('deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B', 
                  cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/models/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B')
"
```

如果 ModelScope 没有这个模型，改用 HuggingFace：
```bash
# 先检查是否有 HF_TOKEN
echo $HF_TOKEN
cat ~/.huggingface/token 2>/dev/null

# DeepSeek 模型通常不需要 token，直接下载
huggingface-cli download deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B \
  --local-dir /root/autodl-tmp/models/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B
```

注意：模型名可能是 `DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B` 或 `DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B`，先 WebSearch 确认 HuggingFace/ModelScope 上的确切 repo 名。

3. 验证下载完整性：
```bash
ls -lh /root/autodl-tmp/models/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B/
# 应该有 config.json, tokenizer.json, safetensors 文件等
python3 -c "from transformers import AutoTokenizer; t = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained('/root/autodl-tmp/models/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B'); print(f'Vocab size: {t.vocab_size}')"
```

4. 更新 vLLM 启动脚本（替换旧的 Qwen 版本脚本）：
在 `/root/symb_invariant_consensus/scripts/start_vllm_deepseek_r1_8b.sh` 创建：
```bash
#!/bin/bash
# vLLM server for DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B (exp-014)
# LLaMA 底座，非 Qwen 家族，用于 Claim 2 跨架构验证
MODEL_PATH="/root/autodl-tmp/models/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B"
python -m vllm.serve "$MODEL_PATH" \
  --port 8003 \
  --tensor-parallel-size 1 \
  --max-model-len 8192 \
  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.90 \
  --dtype auto
```

5. 删除旧的错误模型（可选，节省空间）：
```bash
# 如果磁盘紧张，删除旧的 Qwen 版本
rm -rf /root/autodl-tmp/models/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B
```

**产出**：报告模型路径、大小、验证结果。

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The download is proceeding very slowly - about 400KB/s for each file. This means about 5-7 hours for the full download. There are two issues:

1. The small files' downloads were started. The first download method (small files + aria2c) seems to have completed ("download completed" for 2 files).
2. But the huggingface_hub download (PID 190333) that I killed earlier also seems to have respawned... Wait, no - looking at the log, there's a modelscope lock message. 

Actually I see that the aria2c is not being used here - the download shows "Downloading [model-00001..." which is the huggingface_hub format, not aria2c. 

So aria2c handled the small files but then the HF downloader took over for the rest. The real problem is the speed - 400KB/s is unacceptable. I need to kill everything, clean up, and switch to using aria2c directly with multiple connections on the large files instead of routing through huggingface_hub, pointing it at the mirror URLs.

Looking at the logs more carefully, aria2c finished the small files successfully, but then something triggered the modelscope lock and started single-connection downloads again - I need to figure out what's actually running right now and why the multi-connection approach isn't being used.
```

{
  "server": "westd-32608",
  "command": "cat > /tmp/dl_aria2.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nDEST=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B\"\nBASE=\"https://hf-mirror.com/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B/resolve/main\"\n\necho \"$(date): Starting downloads...\"\n\n# Small files sequentially\nfor f in config.json generation_config.json model.safetensors.index.json tokenizer.json tokenizer_config.json; do\n    echo \"Downloading $f...\"\n    aria2c -x 4 -s 4 -q -d \"$DEST\" -o \"$f\" \"$BASE/$f\"\ndone\necho \"$(date): Small files done\"\n\n# Two large files in parallel, each with 16 connections\necho \"$(date): Starting safetensors downloads...\"\naria2c -x 16 -s 16 -k 20M --file-allocation=none -d \"$DEST\" -o \"model-00001-of-000002.safetensors\" \"$BASE/model-00001-of-000002.safetensors\" &\nPID1=$!\naria2c -x 16 -s 16 -k 20M --file-allocation=none -d \"$DEST\" -o \"model-00002-of-000002.safetensors\" \"$BASE/model-00002-of-000002.safetensors\" &\nPID2=$!\n\necho \"PID1=$PID1, PID2=$PID2\"\nwait $PID1\necho \"$(date): model-00001 done (exit=$?)\"\nwait $PID2\necho \"$(date): model-00002 done (exit=$?)\"\n\necho \"$(date): ALL DONE\"\nls -lh \"$DEST\"/\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /tmp/dl_aria2.sh\nnohup bash /tmp/dl_aria2.sh > /tmp/dl_deepseek.log 2>&1 &\necho \"PID=$!\"",
  "account": "root",
  "warn": true
}

- model-00002: 6.6G (target is 6.89G) — also mostly done with .aria2 file

Both are pre-allocated at their full sizes because aria2c does file allocation. The .aria2 files track which chunks are incomplete. The `du` output was misleading because of the file pre-allocation by aria2c (even though I specified `--file-allocation=none`).


Actually, wait — I did specify `--file-allocation=none`, so these 7.6G and 6.6G figures are the actual downloaded amounts, not pre-allocated space. Both downloads are nearly complete, just a few hundred MB away from their targets.

Hmm, but that's strange — `du -sh` showed only 1.5G total, yet `ls -lh` shows 14.2G combined. The discrepancy makes sense now: `du` reports actual disk blocks used while `ls` shows apparent file size. Since aria2c creates sparse files, the actual data written is around 1.5GB, but the files appear much larger because they're sparse with gaps. If the `.aria2` control files are still present, that indicates the downloads are still in progress.

Looking at the current progress, both files are downloading in parallel with model-00002 at 793MiB/6.8GiB. The combined actual data downloaded is about 1.5GB, and at the current rate of 8.6 MiB/s, I'm looking at roughly 27 minutes remaining to download the remaining 13.5GB.
